  151: 	public static void FillinEnemyHPs()
  152: 	{

  157: 			{
  158: 				try
  159: 				{
  160: 					EnemyHPs.Add(enemyType, enemyType.enemyPrefab.GetComponent<EnemyAI>().enemyHP);
  161: 				}
  162: 				catch (Exception arg)
  163: 				{
  164: 					LethalMin.Logger.LogError((object)$"Failed to get HP for {enemyType.enemyName} due to: {arg}");
  165: 					EnemyHPs.Add(enemyType, 1);
  166: 				}
  167: 			}
  168: 		}
  169: 	}

  171: 	public static PikminLatchTrigger AddLatchTriggerToColider(GameObject go, Transform LookAtObject, PikminEnemy enemy = null)
  172: 	{

  177: 		pikminLatchTrigger.AllowBaseLatchOn = false;

  181: 	}
